<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管AI和深度学习在医学图像分析领域取得显著进展，但许多医疗机构因计算资源有限和缺乏专业技术而难以采用这些技术。

Method: OsteoCAD是一个模块化eHealth框架，提供端到端的深度学习能力（数据集创建、预处理、模型训练和推理），通过集成且用户友好的界面实现，并安全连接到远程GPU基础设施以减轻本地硬件限制。

Result: 通过在墨西哥进行的大骨肿瘤分割真实世界案例研究，验证了该框架的可行性，证明其在无需高级技术专长或复杂本地配置的情况下，能够实现深度学习驱动的eHealth解决方案。

Conclusion: OsteoCAD能够降低深度学习工具在临床实践中普及的门槛，使缺乏先进计算资源和技术人员的医疗机构也能受益于AI驱动的医学图像分析。

Abstract: Artificial Intelligence (AI) and Deep Learning (DL) have notably advanced medical image analysis, yet many health- care organizations struggle to adopt them due to limited com- putational resources and specialized expertise. To address these barriers, we introduce OsteoCAD, a modular eHealth framework that democratizes access to DL tools in clinical practice. Osteo- CAD delivers end-to-end DL capabilities-from dataset creation and preprocessing to model training and inference-through an integrated and user-friendly interface. To mitigate local hardware constraints, the framework securely connects to remote GPU infrastructures. We validate OsteoCAD's feasibility through a real-world case study in Mexico focused on large bone tumor segmentation. The results demonstrate the framework's ability to enable DL-powered eHealth solutions without demanding ad- vanced technical expertise or complex local configurations.

</details>


### [48] [Training-Free Entity-Level Few-Shot Segmentation of Remote Sensing Images with Advection Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29278)
*Xueting Bai,Huan Ni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练的实体级少样本分割框架（ELFSS-AR），通过SAM3生成类别无关的实体基元，并引入平流方程语义细化机制，在不额外训练的情况下有效缓解遥感图像中的域偏移和局部噪声问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨域少样本分割方法存在高训练成本（源域episodic训练和像素级密集预测）以及预测结果碎片化和噪声大的问题，亟需一种低成本且鲁棒的解决方案。

Method: 1) 利用SAM3的通用几何先验生成类别无关的实体基元，将少样本推理从像素级预测重构为实体级推理；2) 构建前景/背景原型并结合SAM3的密集文本语义响应形成多模态语义势场；3) 引入基于平流方程的语义细化机制，在特征空间和相似度空间传播类别感知信息，增强语义连续性并抑制局部纹理噪声。

Result: 在多个遥感数据集上的大量实验表明，该框架有效缓解了域偏移和局部噪声问题，显著提升了SAM3在遥感少样本分割中的适应能力，且无需额外训练。

Conclusion: ELFSS-AR为遥感少样本分割提供了一种训练自由的范式转变，从像素级预测转向实体级推理，结合平流方程细化机制，在低成本条件下实现了优越的跨域分割性能。

Abstract: Existing cross-domain few-shot segmentation approaches suffer from high training costs due to source-domain episodic training and pixel-wise dense prediction, while often producing fragmented and noisy predictions. To overcome these issues, we propose a training-free entity-level few-shot segmentation framework for remote sensing images with advection refinement. Specifically, we first leverage SAM3's generic geometric priors to generate category-agnostic entity primitives. By reformulating few-shot inference from pixel-level prediction to entity-level reasoning, foreground and background prototypes are constructed and combined with dense textual semantic responses from SAM3 to build a multi-modal semantic potential field. Furthermore, an advection equation-based semantic refinement mechanism is introduced to propagate category-aware information across both feature and similarity spaces, enhancing semantic continuity and suppressing local texture noise. Extensive experiments on multiple remote sensing datasets demonstrate that the proposed framework effectively mitigates domain shift and local noise, substantially improving SAM3's adaptation capability for remote sensing few-shot segmentation without additional training. Our code will be publicly available at https://github.com/yu-ni1989/ELFSS-AR.

</details>


### [49] [FillGS: Filling Observation Gaps in 4D Gaussian Splatting via Viewpoint-Time Selection and Generative Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29284)
*Takashi Otonari,Toshihiko Yamasaki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种主动选择时空虚拟视角的管道，用于改进4D高斯泼溅（4DGS）动态场景重建。该方法基于4D高斯的渲染敏感性和运动感知观测密度来选择虚拟视角，优先缓解观测稀疏区域，并在精细图像中过滤不可靠区域后仅用可靠区域微调4DGS。在具有观测间隙的多视角视频基准上，该方法在定性和定量评估中均优于现有视角选择策略和微调方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4DGS在视角覆盖有限时，部分时空区域观测稀疏，尤其在大运动场景中会产生伪影。现有基于生成模型的方法依赖启发式的虚拟视角选择，无法主动探索稀疏观测区域，因此需要一种主动选择视角的策略来提升重建质量。

Method: 提出一个主动选择时空虚拟视角的管道：（1）基于4D高斯的渲染敏感性和运动感知观测密度计算视角优先级，选择能缓解观测稀疏性的虚拟视角；（2）使用生成模型对所选视角进行增强；（3）过滤与真实观测冲突或可能含生成伪影的区域；（4）仅使用可靠区域对4DGS进行微调。

Result: 在带有新训练/测试划分（用于诱导观测间隙）的多视角视频基准上，该方法在定性和定量评估中均较先前的视角选择策略和微调方法取得一致改进，并减少了伪影。

Conclusion: 主动选择虚拟视角而非依赖启发式策略，能有效探索稀疏观测区域，从而显著提升4DGS在大运动动态场景中的重建质量。

Abstract: 4D Gaussian Splatting (4DGS) can render dynamic scenes photorealistically. However, with limited viewpoint coverage, some spatiotemporal regions remain sparsely observed, leading to artifacts, particularly in scenes with large motion. Existing approaches leveraging generative models rely on heuristic virtual-viewpoint selection before refining rendered views. As a result, they cannot actively explore such sparsely observed regions. To address this issue, we propose a pipeline that actively selects spatiotemporal virtual viewpoints to improve 4DGS reconstruction. Our method selects virtual viewpoints for generative enhancement based on the rendering sensitivity and motion-aware observation density of 4D Gaussians, prioritizing views that alleviate observation sparsity. In the refined images, we filter out regions that conflict with captured observations or are likely to contain generative artifacts and then fine-tune 4DGS using only the reliable regions. We evaluate our method on multi-view video benchmarks using new train/test splits designed to induce observation gaps. Results show consistent improvements over prior viewpoint selection strategies and fine-tuning methods in both qualitative and quantitative evaluations, while reducing artifacts.

</details>


### [50] [CALM-AH: An ABAW11-Calibrated Multimodal Ensemble with Reliability-Gated Multi-Expert Consensus for Video-Level Ambivalence and Hesitancy Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29310)
*Wenzhuo Sun,Mingjian Liang,Richard Attfield,Zongyuan Ge,Xuelian Cheng,Pamela Carreno-Medrano*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CALM-AH多模态集成系统及RG-MEC基于可靠性门控的多专家共识框架，用于ABAW11 A/H视频识别挑战赛，在参与者独立的验证集上达到0.7771的Macro-F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矛盾心理和犹豫（A/H）是微妙的行为状态，可通过语言、语音、面部活动等多种线索表达。ABAW11挑战要求系统在每个自然化访谈视频上输出二元A/H标签，并以Macro-F1度量性能，确保两类样本识别同等重要。挑战在于单一模态特征难以充分捕捉A/H的微妙表现，且需要有效融合多模态信息并控制个别专家错误的负面影响。

Method: 提出CALM-AH多模态集成为基础系统，融合文本、声学、视觉及派生行为统计特征，构建15种非空特征分支组合，每种组合从三个分类器家族中基于验证集交叉熵选择最优者并优化决策阈值，最终用BROTHER迁移的固定硬投票权重融合。进一步提出RG-MEC（Reliability-Gated Multi-Expert Consensus）决策级集成框架，以初始预测为锚点，仅当CALM-AH、AffectGPT和GPT语义验证器三个修正专家一致同意同一替代类别时才覆盖初始标签，否则保留锚定预测。这种一致性门控设计限制个别专家错误的影响，同时允许在任务特定、多模态情感和语义语用证据完全一致时进行双向修正。

Result: 在参与者独立的ABAW11数据集上，CALM-AH达到0.7525的Macro-F1，完整RG-MEC系统达到0.7771。

Conclusion: 通过多模态集成和基于一致性门控的多专家决策融合，能够有效提升A/H识别的整体性能，相比单一集成系统，RG-MEC框架带来了约0.025的Macro-F1提升。

Abstract: Ambivalence and hesitancy (A/H) are subtle behavioural states that may be expressed through language, voice, facial activity, and other non-verbal cues. The ABAW11 A/H Video Recognition Challenge asks systems to assign a binary A/H label to each naturalistic interview video. Performance is measured using Macro-F1 so that recognition of both A/H and No-A/H samples receives equal importance. We present CALM-AH, a multimodal ensemble that combines textual, acoustic, visual, and derived behavioural-statistical features. We construct 15 non-empty combinations of these feature branches. For each combination, we select the best of three classifier families using validation binary cross-entropy and optimise its decision threshold for validation Macro-F1. The resulting binary decisions are combined using fixed hard-voting weights transferred from BROTHER. We further introduce Reliability-Gated Multi-Expert Consensus(RG-MEC), an anchor-preserving decision-level ensemble that combines an initial prediction with three complementary correction experts: CALM-AH, AffectGPT, and a GPT-based semantic verifier. The initial system provides the default prediction. Its label is overridden only when all three correction experts unanimously support the same alternative class; otherwise, the anchor prediction is retained. This unanimity-gated design limits the influence of isolated expert errors while permitting bidirectional correction when task-specific, multimodal-affective, and semantic-pragmatic evidence are fully consistent. On the participant-disjoint ABAW11 dataset, CALM-AH achieves a Macro-F1 of 0.7525, and the complete RG-MEC system achieves 0.7771.

</details>


### [51] [DualDiT: A Conditional Dual-Output Diffusion Transformer for Joint OCT Image and Segmentation Mask Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29337)
*Fernando García-Torres,Rocío del Amor,Sandra Morales,Álvaro Barroso,Peter Heiduschka,Björn Kemper,Valery Naranjo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种条件双输出扩散Transformer（DualDiT），用于联合合成小鼠眼OCT B-scan图像及其视网膜上层细胞层的分割掩码，通过预训练VAE将两种模态编码到共享潜空间并进行条件扩散。实验表明DualDiT在生成质量（FID 56.14, sFID 114.35）、下游分割任务增益和专家感知真实度方面均优于DDPM和LDM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OCT小鼠眼底视网膜分层标注高度依赖专业知识且劳动密集，导致标注数据稀缺。虽然扩散模型在医学图像合成中表现良好，但联合图像-掩码生成主要依赖U-Net去噪器，扩散Transformer在该领域尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出DualDiT模型：将OCT图像和分割掩码两种模态通过预训练VAE编码到共享潜空间，拼接其潜在表征，并在联合张量上执行条件扩散。模型以扩散Transformer为去噪器，实现双输出联合生成。对比基线为DDPM和LDM，评估维度包括生成质量（FID/sFID）、下游U-Net分割的增强效用及三位领域专家的感知评估。

Result: DualDiT在生成质量上最优（FID 56.14, sFID 114.35），超越DDPM和LDM。专家评估中46%的合成样本被误判为真实，42%的真实样本被误判为合成。使用DualDiT生成的图像和掩码进行数据增强后，分割测试集上的Dice和IoU均有所提升。

Conclusion: DualDiT证明了基于Transformer的扩散模型能够有效学习OCT图像与分割掩码的联合分布，在生成保真度、下游实用性和感知真实度方面均优于DDPM和LDM基线，展示了其在标注稀缺医学影像场景中作为数据增强工具的潜力。

Abstract: Background and Objective: Generating realistic medical images with anatomically accurate segmentation masks helps address the shortage of annotated data in medical imaging, particularly in optical coherence tomography (OCT) of mouse eyes, where manual retinal layer delineation is labour-intensive due to tiny structures and required expertise, resulting in scarce datasets. While diffusion models perform well in medical image synthesis, joint image-mask generation has relied mainly on U-Net-based denoisers, leaving diffusion transformers largely unexplored. Methods: We propose a conditional dual-output Diffusion Transformer (DualDiT) for joint synthesis of OCT B-scans and segmentation masks of the upper retinal cell layers in ex vivo mouse retina. DualDiT encodes both modalities into a shared latent space via a pretrained VAE, concatenates their latent representations, and performs conditional diffusion over the joint tensor. We compared DualDiT against two adapted diffusion baselines: DDPM and LDM. Generative quality was assessed via Fréchet Inception Distance (FID) and spatial FID (sFID); practical utility via synthetic data augmentation for downstream U-Net segmentation; and perceptual realism via evaluation by three domain experts. Results: DualDiT achieved the best generative quality (FID 56.14, sFID 114.35), outperforming DDPM and LDM. Expert panels misclassified 46% of synthetic samples as real and 42% of real samples as synthetic. Adding DualDiT-generated images and masks improved Dice and IoU scores on a held-out segmentation test set. Conclusions: DualDiT shows that transformer-based diffusion models can effectively learn the joint distribution of OCT images and segmentation masks, surpassing DDPM- and LDM-based baselines in generative fidelity, downstream utility, and perceptual realism, highlighting its potential for data augmentation in annotation-scarce medical imaging.

</details>


### [52] [SatEdit: Mask-Conditioned Image Editing via VLM-Guided Segment Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29367)
*Muhammad Talha,Muhammad Ahmed Amer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SatEdit是一个掩码条件化的卫星图像编辑框架，利用分割基础模型提出目标掩码、视觉语言模型分配语义标签，并结合轻量人工验证构建训练数据，最终通过LoRA微调高分辨率图像编辑骨干网络，在1,014张图像和852个验证目标注释上进行训练，实现了最高的掩码区域语义对齐性能（CLIP分数0.6322，CLIP delta 0.0726）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星图像编辑需要空间上精确的对象级控制，但有监督的编辑数据集构建成本极高，因为对象掩码、语义标签和配对编辑在大规模上很少可用。

Method: 提出SatEdit框架：(1) 使用分割基础模型提出对象掩码；(2) 使用视觉语言模型（VLM）为采样片段分配语义标签；(3) 进行轻量人工验证；(4) 通过掩码引导的修复生成成对的添加和删除示例；(5) 使用LoRA在SODA-A衍生数据集上微调高分辨率图像编辑骨干网络。

Result: 在与开源和专有图像编辑模型的对比中，SatEdit取得了最高的聚合掩码区域语义对齐，CLIP分数为0.6322，CLIP delta为0.0726，同时在定性上保留了周围场景。

Conclusion: VLM辅助的片段标注是数据高效、空间可控的卫星图像编辑的一条实用路径。

Abstract: Satellite image editing requires spatially precise object-level control, but supervised editing datasets for overhead imagery are costly to build because object masks, semantic labels, and paired edits are rarely available at scale. We introduce SatEdit, a mask-conditioned satellite image editing framework that constructs training supervision from unlabeled imagery. SatEdit proposes object masks with a seg- mentation foundation model, assigns semantic la- bels to sampled segments with a Vision-Language Model, and applies lightweight human verification before generating paired addition and removal exam- ples through mask-guided inpainting. We fine-tune a high-resolution image editing backbone with LoRA on a SODA-A-derived dataset containing 1,014 im- ages and 852 verified object annotations across 91 classes. In controlled comparisons with open- source and proprietary image editing models, SatE- dit achieves the highest aggregate masked-region se- mantic alignment, with a CLIP score of 0.6322 and CLIP delta of 0.0726, while preserving the surround- ing scene qualitatively. These results suggest that VLM-assisted segment annotation is a practical route to data-efficient, spatially controllable satellite image editing.

</details>


### [53] [VFAD: Variational Semantic Prompting Meets Frequency-Adaptive Representation Learning for Zero-Shot Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29370)
*Peng Chen,Kaige Li,Wei Wang,Mingbo Yang,Wenqiang Wang,Li Shen,Fangjun Huang,Chao Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VFAD是一个统一的零样本异常检测框架，结合变分语义提示与频率自适应表示学习，通过变分语义提示提取器（VSPE）和频率自适应表示聚合模块（FARA）分别增强跨模态对齐和异常判别性视觉表示，在13个工业和医学基准上持续优于现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于CLIP的零样本异常检测（ZSAD）方法虽然通过视觉-语言对齐展现了一定的泛化能力，但在捕捉多样化的异常语义和细微的局部变化方面存在局限，难以实现精确的异常判别与定位。

Method: 提出VFAD框架，包含两个核心模块：（1）变分语义提示提取器（VSPE），从密集patch标记中自适应聚合异常相关的局部语义，并通过变分信息瓶颈进行正则化，实现细粒度视觉线索的融合与精确的跨模态对齐；（2）频率自适应表示聚合模块（FARA），利用小波基频率分解和频率特定专家聚合来增强异常判别性的视觉表示。两者联合强化语义引导和视觉表示学习。

Result: 在13个工业和医学基准数据集上的大量实验表明，VFAD在多种异常场景下持续优于现有的最先进ZSAD方法。

Conclusion: 通过同时增强语义引导和视觉表示学习，VFAD在异常判别和细粒度定位方面均取得显著改进，验证了变分语义提示与频率自适应表示学习相结合的有效性。

Abstract: Zero-shot anomaly detection (ZSAD) aims to detect and localize anomalies in unseen categories without access to target-specific training data. Although recent CLIP-based methods have demonstrated promising generalization through vision-language alignment, they remain limited in capturing diverse anomaly semantics and subtle local variations. To address these limitations, we propose VFAD, a unified framework that combines variational semantic prompting with frequency-adaptive representation learning. Specifically, we introduce a Variational Semantic Prompt Extractor (VSPE), which adaptively aggregates anomaly-relevant local semantics from dense patch tokens and regularizes them through a variational information bottleneck, thereby incorporating fine-grained visual cues and enabling more precise cross-modal alignment. Furthermore, we develop a Frequency-Adaptive Representation Aggregation (FARA) module that leverages wavelet-based frequency decomposition and frequency-specific expert aggregation to enhance anomaly-discriminative visual representations. By jointly strengthening semantic guidance and visual representation learning, VFAD improves both anomaly discrimination and fine-grained localization. Extensive experiments on 13 industrial and medical benchmarks demonstrate that VFAD consistently outperforms existing state-of-the-art ZSAD methods across diverse anomaly scenarios. The code will be publicly available upon publication.

</details>


### [54] [Dense Temporal Contrast Synthesis via Conditioned Latent Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29394)
*Smriti Joshi,Apostolia Tsirikoglou,Daniel M. Lang,Richard Osuala,Noah Márquez Varaa,Alejandro Guzman,Grzegorz Skorupko,Sebastian Ibarra Arregui,Lidia Garrucho,Akane Ohashi,Dimitra Ntoula,Eugen Divjak,Oğuz Lafcı,Jan C. Peeken,Julia A. Schnabel,Fredrik Strand,Oliver Diaz,Karim Lekadir*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种条件潜空间传输框架（conditioned latent transport framework），通过单次前向传播预测DCE-MRI中的对比增强序列，无需注射钆基造影剂。该方法锚定潜空间轨迹到增强前解剖结构并施加连续时间条件，在外部独立队列上展现出对域偏移的鲁棒性，且合成对比增强显著提升下游肿瘤分割性能（Dice系数相对提升22.4%），并通过四位乳腺放射科医生的读片研究验证了其临床可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DCE-MRI对乳腺癌管理至关重要，但依赖含钆造影剂（GBCAs）存在三重限制：禁忌人群无法使用、延长扫描流程、以及环境毒性问题。现有对比合成方法在空间真实性与时间连续性之间难以平衡，迭代采样速度慢，未充分利用结构先验信息，且缺乏临床验证。因此需要一种非侵入性、快速且临床可靠的对比增强合成方法。

Method: 提出条件潜空间传输框架：1）将潜空间轨迹锚定到增强前（pre-contrast）解剖结构上，充分利用结构先验；2）应用连续时间条件（continuous time conditioning），使模型能够在任意采集时间点合成患者特异的对比增强演变；3）通过单次前向传播（single forward pass）完成预测，避免了迭代采样的计算瓶颈。

Result: 该方法在空间、感知、时间及分布指标上均优于基线及最先进模型。在外部独立队列上对扫描噪声和采集协议差异引起的域偏移具有鲁棒性。合成对比增强使下游肿瘤分割的Dice系数从0.49提升至0.60（相对提升22.4%，p<0.01），边界分割误差降低超过39%。读片研究中，四位乳腺放射科医生在40例随机病例中评估发现，70%的合成图像足以支持与真实DCE-MRI相同的临床管理决策。

Conclusion: 该条件潜空间传输框架为无造影剂或减少造影剂的DCE-MRI工作流提供了可行路径，在图像质量、动力学保真度及诊断可行性上达到临床可接受水平，具有实现更安全、更快速乳腺MRI检查的潜力。

Abstract: Dynamic contrast-enhanced magnetic resonance imaging (DCE-MRI) is essential for breast cancer management, but reliance on gadolinium-based contrast agents (GBCAs) restricts use in contraindicated populations, prolongs scan protocols, and presents environmental toxicity concerns. Contrast synthesis offers a non-invasive alternative; however, existing approaches struggle to balance spatial realism with temporal continuity, suffer from slow iterative sampling, underutilize structural priors, and lack clinical validation. We propose a novel conditioned latent transport framework that predicts contrast enhancement in a single forward pass. By anchoring the latent trajectory to the pre-contrast anatomy and applying continuous time conditioning, the model synthesizes patient-specific contrast evolution at any acquisition time. The proposed approach outperforms baseline and the state-of-the-art models across spatial, perceptual, temporal, and distributional metrics. Evaluated on an independent external cohort, the method demonstrates robustness to domain shifts induced by scanner noise as well as differing acquisition protocol. Furthermore, our synthetic contrast enhancement significantly improved downstream tumor segmentation performance, yielding a 22.4% relative increase in Dice coefficient (0.60 vs. 0.49 baseline pre-contrast, p < 0.01), reducing boundary segmentation error by over 39%, while outperforming all other generative model baselines. Finally, a reader study involving four breast radiologists evaluated the image quality, kinetic fidelity, and diagnostic viability of our synthesized sequences across 40 randomly selected cases. The results demonstrated that in 70% of cases, synthesized images provided sufficient clinical information to support the same management decisions as real DCE-MRI, suggesting a path toward safer and faster contrast-free or contrast-reduced imaging workflows.

</details>


### [55] [OSEF: One-Step Evidence Fusion for Cross-Video Scene Procedure Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29401)
*Zhentong Ye,Lei Zhang,Sijia Zhou,Yingda Yu,Yuehan Shi,Jiaqi Xuan,Shuaiwu Dong,Guanchao Tong,Meimei Zhang,Bin Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出跨视频场景流程规划（CVSPP）任务：给定答案遮盖的起始-目标查询和K个候选视频，模型需检索支持视频、定位相关窗口并预测动作序列。作者构建了含11个来源的基准，并提出One-Step Evidence Fusion（OSEF）方法，通过查询条件下的单元格-跨度网格评分并利用token-global适配器将完整软网格输入规划器，无需预先裁剪窗口。OSEF在所有6个可排名单元上排名第一，在4个COIN/CrossTask匹配单元上提升精确视频与规划成功率2.9-10.7个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频场景流程规划（VSPP）假设起始-目标观测已给定，未考虑证据本身需要被检索的场景。现实中模型往往面对的是未标注的视频池，需先检索证据再规划，而硬选择（hard selection）会在早期丢失正确的场景链，导致规划失败。作者旨在解决证据检索与规划耦合中的两个障碍：同一任务演示共享阶段和窗口，以及早期硬选择传递错误场景链的问题。

Method: 作者提出One-Step Evidence Fusion (OSEF)方法：对所有候选视频构建查询条件下的单元格-跨度网格（cell-and-span lattice），评分全部候选而不预先裁剪，然后通过token-global适配器将完整软网格（soft lattice）馈送给规划器。基准构建方面，作者设计了11个来源的数据集，包含类型化负角色（typed negative roles）和防漏答案门控（fail-closed answer-leakage gate），并分别从证据轴（Evidence-axis）和规划轴（Plan-axis）两个维度进行指标评估。

Result: OSEF在基准认证为可方法排名的全部6个单元上位列第一。在4个匹配的同任务COIN与CrossTask单元上，相比增强的硬选择SOTA方法，OSEF在精确视频-规划联合成功率上提升2.9-10.7个百分点。消融研究表明最大单项增益来自token-global接口。5个转换来源单元处于或接近多数序列基线，表明基准仍有提升空间。

Conclusion: 软证据融合而非硬选择是解决CVSPP的关键，将检索到的证据以完整软网格形式透传到规划器可避免早期错误传播。目前基准中部分转换来源单元接近多数序列基线，说明这些单元尚未提供有效的区分性挑战，是未来的改进方向。

Abstract: Video Scene Procedure Planning (VSPP) supplies the target start-goal observations in advance, leaving open how a planner should act when the evidence must itself be retrieved. We introduce Cross-Video Scene Procedure Planning (CVSPP): given an answer-redacted start-goal query and K candidate videos, a model must retrieve the supporting video, localize the relevant window, and predict the action sequence. Two obstacles couple here. Same-task demonstrations share stages and windows, and an early hard selection passes the wrong scene chain to the planner. We build an eleven-source benchmark with typed negative roles, a fail-closed answer-leakage gate, and separate Evidence- and Plan-axis metrics. On its 14 source-horizon cells we adapt nine planner families against a majority-sequence floor. We then present One-Step Evidence Fusion (OSEF), which scores a query-conditioned cell-and-span lattice over all candidates and feeds the full soft lattice to the planner through a token-global adapter, cropping no window beforehand. OSEF ranks first on all six cells the benchmark certifies as method-rankable. On four matched same-task COIN and CrossTask cells it improves exact-video-and-plan success by 2.9-10.7 points over an enhanced hard-selection SOTA, and a component study assigns the largest single increment to the token-global interface. Five converted-source cells sit at or near the majority-sequence floor, the benchmark's remaining headroom. The supplementary package includes model constructors and evaluation code.

</details>


### [56] [Role-Break in Attention Heads: Understanding and Detecting Hallucinations in VLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29412)
*Mingyu Wang,Weilin Jin,Wenbo Li,Haoyang Huang,Nan Duan,Tong Jia,Chaoran Luo,Ying Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于统一头部视角的幻觉检测方法，发现VLM幻觉表现为注意力头行为偏离正常角色（Role-Break），基于此构建无需微调VLM的轻量线性检测器，特征维度低于5000，在六个VLM和四个基准上达到93.23的平均AUROC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM幻觉检测或缓解方法大多针对单一幻觉模式（如视觉-文本不平衡）设计，但真实幻觉往往是多种模式的混合，导致单一信号在不同模型和任务间难以保持稳定。

Method: 1) 从统一头部视角出发，提出Role-Break现象，即幻觉引起的注意力头行为偏差表现为与各头忠实上下文行为的局部偏离；2) 详细分析发现这些偏差在注意力头、上下文来源和偏差方向上系统性地组织；3) 在保留头部身份的前提下构建轻量线性检测器，无需微调VLM，特征维度低于5000。

Result: 所提出的检测器在六个VLM和四个基准上达到93.23的平均AUROC，超越了依赖单一幻觉模式信号的方法，且小规模干预实验表明检测到的token可以直接在判别设置中应用。

Conclusion: Role-Break提供了一种统一、稳定且可读的幻觉信号，支持构建轻量级、无需微调的高性能幻觉检测器，并可为后续干预缓解提供可操作的依据。

Abstract: Despite remarkable progress in vision-language generation, Vision-Language Models (VLMs) remain prone to hallucinations, producing content that is inconsistent with or unsupported by the input image. Existing works largely design detection or mitigation methods around one specific hallucination pattern, such as visual-textual imbalance, but real VLM hallucinations arise from a mixture of multiple patterns, so signals bound to a single pattern struggle to remain stable across models and tasks. Under a unified head-level view, we find that hallucination-induced changes manifest as localized deviations from each head's faithful contextual behavior, a phenomenon we term Role-Break. Detailed analysis reveals that these deviations are systematically organized across attention heads, contextual sources, and deviation directions, and that the resulting signal is linearly readable once head identity is preserved. Based on these findings, we build a lightweight linear detector on top of Role-Break that requires no fine-tuning of the VLM, whose feature dimension stays below 5,000 and reaches an average AUROC of 93.23 across six VLMs and four benchmarks. A small-scale intervention experiment further shows that the detected tokens can be directly acted upon in the discriminative setting.

</details>


### [57] [QR-Structured Thermal Triggers for Targeted Semantic Attacks on Infrared Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29445)
*Xiang Chen,Yingying Zhao,Chao Li,Jiaju Han,Ben Zhang,Ang Li,Jiahuan Long,Yiwei Wei,Jiujiang Guo,Chengyin Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出QR-STT（QR结构化热触发），一种无需训练、黑盒的隐蔽攻击框架，可在红外视觉语言模型（IR-VLMs）上实现目标语义定向操控。通过优化QR码内部模块的热状态（冷/中/热）及渲染参数，QR-STT在保持视觉隐蔽性的同时，重定向图像-文本对齐，且攻击可跨任务迁移至图像描述和视觉问答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 红外视觉语言模型（IR-VLMs）已扩展至开放词汇分类、图像描述和视觉问答等任务，但其对结构化热扰动的鲁棒性以及跨模态语义对齐的稳定性研究不足，缺乏对黑盒、训练无关攻击面的系统探索。

Method: QR-STT框架：（1）保留QR码功能区域的内部模块结构，为每个模块分配冷、中性或热三种热状态；（2）联合搜索模块拓扑和渲染参数（位置、尺度、旋转、强度、模糊、圆度）；（3）采用三阶段无梯度优化流程配合贪婪模块翻转精化，处理混合离散-连续搜索空间；（4）目标函数同时促进与攻击者选择目标的语义对齐、抑制源类别证据、并正则化QR结构和视觉相似性。

Result: 在多个CLIP风格编码器上的实验表明，QR-STT能持续将图像-文本对齐重定向至选定概念，同时保持视觉隐蔽性。针对分类优化的扰动可迁移至图像描述和视觉问答任务，导致生成输出出现与目标一致的语义漂移。

Conclusion: QR结构化热模式是红外视觉语言模型的一个可解释攻击面，凸显了对跨任务结构语义攻击进行鲁棒性评估的必要性。

Abstract: Infrared vision-language models (IR-VLMs) extend thermal perception to open-vocabulary classification, image captioning, and visual question answering. However, their robustness to structured thermal perturbations and the stability of cross-modal semantic alignment remain insufficiently studied. We propose QR-Structured Thermal Triggers (QR-STT), a stealthy, training-free, black-box framework for targeted semantic steering of IR-VLMs. QR-STT preserves the functional regions of a QR pattern while optimizing its internal modules, each of which is assigned a cold, neutral, or hot thermal state. The framework jointly searches module topology and rendering parameters, including position, scale, rotation, intensity, blur, and roundness. A three-stage gradient-free procedure with greedy module-flip refinement efficiently handles the mixed discrete and continuous search space. The objective promotes alignment with an attacker-selected target, suppresses source-class evidence, and regularizes QR structure and visual similarity. Experiments on multiple CLIP-style encoders show that QR-STT consistently redirects image-text alignment toward chosen concepts while maintaining visual stealth. Perturbations optimized for classification also transfer to image captioning and VQA, causing target-consistent semantic drift in generated outputs. These results identify QR-structured thermal patterns as an interpretable attack surface for language-driven infrared perception and highlight the need for robustness evaluation against structured cross-task semantic attacks.

</details>


### [58] [Weight-Space Mixture-of-Experts for Implicit Neural Representation Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29463)
*Stanislaw Janik,Michal Byra*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分层混合专家（HMoE）Transformer用于在隐式神经表示（INR）权重空间中直接进行分类，并结合元学习框架塑造INR参数。该方法在多个基准上（包括ImageNet-1K）达到SOTA精度，同时通过权重空间归因和剪枝方法揭示了INR中类别特定信息的分布结构，增强了权重空间学习的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: INR将信号编码为坐标神经网络的权重，作为下游学习的新领域具有潜力，但直接在权重空间进行分类面临两个核心挑战：一是INR参数维度高、结构复杂；二是对判别信息在INR权重中的分布方式理解不足。现有方法缺乏针对INR结构特点设计的分类模型及相应的可解释性工具。

Method: 提出一个分层混合专家（HMoE）Transformer，关键设计包括：(1) 采用基于底层隐式网络结构的条件计算，使MoE架构与INR的层次结构对齐；(2) 结合元学习框架来塑造INR参数，使其更适合下游分类任务；(3) 开发权重空间归因和剪枝方法，用于识别对分类最关键的参数，从而分析INR如何编码判别性信息。

Result: 该方法在从低分辨率数据集到高分辨率ImageNet-1K的标准基准上均达到最先进（SOTA）精度。归因和剪枝分析揭示了类别特定结构如何在INR层内涌现，验证了MoE架构用于权重空间学习的适配性。

Conclusion: 本文同时提升了权重空间分类器的性能与可解释性，证明了将MoE条件计算与INR结构对齐并结合元学习塑造参数空间的有效性，为理解INR内部信息编码机制提供了新的分析工具。

Abstract: Implicit Neural Representations (INRs) encode signals as the weights of a coordinate-based neural network and have recently been proposed as an alternative domain for downstream learning. While promising, classification directly in weight space remains challenging due to the high dimensionality and complex structure of INR parameters. Furthermore, the way discriminative information is distributed across INR weights remains poorly understood. We propose a hierarchical Mixture-of-Experts (HMoE) Transformer that processes INR weights using conditional computation aligned with the structure of the underlying implicit network. Coupled with a meta-learning framework that shapes INR parameters for downstream tasks, our model achieves state-of-the-art accuracy across standard benchmarks, ranging from low-resolution datasets to high-resolution ImageNet-1K. To gain insight into how INRs encode discriminative information, we develop weight-space attribution and pruning methods that identify parameters most relevant for classification. These analyses reveal how class-specific structure emerges within INR layers and support the suitability of MoE architectures for weight-space learning. Our approach advances both the performance and interpretability of weight-space classifiers.

</details>


### [59] [Lightweight Neural Networks for Affordance Segmentation: Enhancement of the Decoder Module](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29473)
*Simone Lugani,Edoardo Ragusa,Rodolfo Zunino,Paolo Gastaldo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文分析了可穿戴机器人上视觉可供性分割任务中分割头在泛化性能与计算成本权衡中的作用。结果表明，所提出的模型在真实世界数据集上优于现代基线方案，同时满足低计算需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可穿戴机器人上的视觉可供性分割面临一个矛盾：可供性分割需要高层抽象能力（通常依赖大型模型），但可穿戴机器人上的计算资源无法实时运行大型模型。因此需要研究如何在模型规模受限的情况下仍保持良好的分割性能。

Method: 论文重点分析了分割头（segmentation head）在整个模型中所扮演的角色，研究其对泛化性能和计算成本之间权衡的影响，从而在保证低计算开销的前提下获得较好的泛化能力。

Result: 所提出的模型在知名的真实世界数据集上超越了现代基线解决方案，同时满足了低计算要求，实现了性能与效率之间的良好平衡。

Conclusion: 分割头的设计选择对可穿戴机器人上可供性分割的"性能-计算"权衡有显著影响，通过优化分割头可以在保持实时运行能力的同时达到优于基线的分割效果。

Abstract: The deployment of deep neural networks for visual affordance segmentation on wearable robots poses may prove critical, due to some conflicting aspects of the problem. On one hand, affordance segmentation requires high-level abstraction capabilities, that typically involve large-size models. On the other hand, computing resources hosted on wearable robots prevent to run large-size models in real-time. The paper presents an analysis of the role of the segmentation head in the trade-off between generalization performance and compute cost. The obtained models outperform modern baseline solutions in well-known, real-world datasets while meeting low computing requirements.

</details>


### [60] [Leveraging Transfer Learning with Class-Specific Decoders for Laparoscopic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29509)
*Priya Tomar,Aditya Parikh,Christian Bauckhage,Rafet Sifa*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了跨手术领域（直肠手术和胆囊切除术）下多器官分割中的知识迁移，扩展了类特定解码器架构（CEMD）以探索编码器和解码器在不同训练阶段的特征适应和知识保留。结果表明，跨域预训练后完全微调的器官特定解码器模型CEMD达到了最高的分割性能（62.4% Dice），且收敛速度显著快于从头训练，但类别不平衡问题在迁移学习下仍未能完全解决。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腹腔镜多器官分割面临两个核心挑战：学习复杂的解剖特征以及缓解类别不平衡问题（小型和暴露有限的结构占比较低）。现有工作聚焦于通过类特定解码器架构学习结构特定特征，但尚未充分探索跨手术领域的知识共享。

Method: 利用两个代表不同手术领域的公开数据集（直肠手术和胆囊切除术），在部分共同的解剖表征下探索手术概念知识的迁移。研究中对比了编码器和解码器在不同训练阶段的特征适应方式，分析了网络中的知识适应与保留，并将跨域预训练后的CEMD模型（类特定解码器架构）与从头训练进行对比。

Result: 完全微调后的CEMD模型（跨域预训练后）获得最高分割性能（62.4% Dice），且收敛速度显著快于从头训练，验证了先前关于解码器特定架构的研究发现。但类别不平衡仍是迁移学习未能完全解决的持续挑战，尤其是对于代表性不足的解剖结构。

Conclusion: 跨域预训练结合类特定解码器架构（CEMD）能够提升腹腔镜多器官分割性能并加速收敛，但迁移学习无法根本解决手术数据中固有的类别不平衡问题，该问题仍需后续研究关注。

Abstract: Effective multi-organ segmentation in surgical data requires learning the intricate anatomical features and alleviating the challenge of class imbalance, which results from relatively lower proportions of small and limitedly exposed structures. Recent works on laparoscopic multi-organ segmentation focus on learning structure-specific features through class-specific decoder architectures and report favorable results. This work extends the decoder-focused architectures to investigate knowledge sharing in the cross-surgical domain. We utilize two datasets representing different surgical domains, rectal and cholecystectomy surgeries, to explore how surgical conceptual knowledge transfers under partially common anatomical representations. Additionally, we compare the feature adaptation for the encoder and decoder at different training stages to analyse the knowledge adaptation and retention in the network. Our results corroborate previous findings on decoder-specific architectures and demonstrate that the organ-specific decoder model (CEMD), fully fine-tuned after cross-domain pre-training, achieves the highest segmentation performance (62.4\% dice) while converging substantially faster than training from scratch. However, we also find that class imbalance in surgical data remains a persistent challenge that transfer learning does not fully resolve for underrepresented anatomical structures.

</details>


### [61] [Multi-Source Multi-View Graph Domain Adaptation with Hyperbolic Residual Encoding for Cross-Site MDD Identification from rs-fMRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29531)
*Zhanpeng Zheng,Xiran Chen,Haiteng Jiang,Renjie Tian,Qinyu Cai,Jiexi Liu,Xiaofeng Chen,Weikai Li,Yansu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种跨站点抑郁障碍（MDD）分类框架，联合建模多种功能连接视角并采用多源无监督域适应，在七个未见目标域上达到73.60%的平均准确率和71.90%的AUC。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨站点MDD分类受制于站点间分布偏移和异构功能连接（FC）视角的差异。现有研究大多将多视角连接组学学习与跨站点适应分开处理，很少联合建模多个FC视角下的多源无监督域适应问题。

Method: 构建皮尔逊相关、稀疏表示和格兰杰因果三种图，各由视角特异的图注意力网络编码；双流自适应融合显式整合成对跨视角交互；轻量双曲残差编码实现曲率感知表示细化；类级Cauchy-Schwarz对齐减少源间及源目标域差异，辅以对抗学习、信息最大化和置信度感知伪标签。

Result: 在七个未见标签的目标域上，框架达到73.60%的平均准确率和71.90%的AUC，展现了对异质采集条件的有效泛化能力。

Conclusion: 统一异质视角建模、曲率感知细化与多源域适应相结合的方法，可有效提升跨站点MDD识别性能。

Abstract: Cross-site identification of major depressive disorder (MDD) from resting-state functional magnetic resonance imaging (rs-fMRI) is hindered by inter-site distribution shifts and heterogeneous functional connectivity (FC) views. These views capture complementary neural relationships but exhibit distinct site biases and graph topologies, complicating alignment without sacrificing disease-relevant information or cross-view consistency. Existing studies largely treat multi-view connectome learning and cross-site adaptation separately. To the best of our knowledge, few studies have jointly modeled multiple FC views under multi-source unsupervised domain adaptation for cross-site rs-fMRI-based MDD classification. We construct Pearson correlation, sparse representation, and Granger causality graphs, each encoded by a view-specific graph attention network. Dual-stream adaptive fusion explicitly integrates pairwise cross-view interactions, followed by lightweight hyperbolic residual encoding for curvature-aware representation refinement. Class-wise Cauchy--Schwarz alignment reduces inter-source and source-target discrepancies, complemented by adversarial learning, information maximization, and confidence-aware pseudo-labeling. Across seven unlabeled target domains, our framework achieves 73.60% mean accuracy and 71.90% AUC, demonstrating effective generalization under heterogeneous acquisition conditions. These results highlight the effectiveness of unified heterogeneous-view modeling, curvature-aware refinement, and multi-source domain adaptation for cross-site MDD identification.The source code is at https://github.com/OPUS-Lightphenexx/MM-HyperGDA

</details>


### [62] [OSAGEN: Object-Aware Mask Priors and Multistage Decoupled Diffusion for Industrial Anomaly Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29533)
*Jinyi Xu,Peng Chen,Yunkang Cao,Chengliang Liu,Xinghui Dong,Chao Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OSAGEN是一种用于工业异常检测与定位的少样本掩码引导生成方法，结合对象感知掩码先验与多阶段解耦扩散，在MVTec AD和VisA数据集上取得了显著的检测与定位性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业异常检测与定位受限于真实的异常样本稀缺以及像素级标注成本高昂。虽然合成图像-掩码对可以缓解这一问题，但现有的少样本掩码引导生成方法存在过度跟随掩码几何形状、生成异常过于微弱、以及条件掩码与当前目标实例不兼容等问题。

Method: OSAGEN提出三阶段适应机制：第一阶段学习正常外观，第二阶段在粗粒度条件下学习缺陷外观，第三阶段进行细粒度掩码校准。其核心组件包括QBG（query-based generation），通过从匹配的正常图像中注入对象结构到掩码扩散中以生成对象感知先验；以及ISC（instance-aware sampling constraint），在采样过程中限制异常传播并保留正常内容。最后通过轻量级的物化步骤恢复与真实缺陷对齐的像素级标签。

Result: 在统一的后续定位协议下，OSAGEN在MVTec AD和VisA上分别取得了AP-P/F1-P分数88.1/82.2和68.5/66.1。

Conclusion: OSAGEN通过对象感知掩码先验与多阶段解耦扩散的组合，有效解决了现有少样本掩码引导生成方法的缺陷，在工业异常检测与定位任务上展现了优越性能。代码将在论文被接收后发布。

Abstract: Industrial anomaly detection and localization are limited by scarce real anomalies and pixel-level annotations, a bottleneck that synthetic image-mask pairs can alleviate. However, existing few-shot mask-guided generation may over-follow mask geometry, produce weak anomalies, or use condition masks incompatible with the current object instance. We propose OSAGEN, which combines object-aware mask priors with multistage decoupled diffusion. Its three-stage adaptation sequentially learns normal appearance, defect appearance under coarse conditions, and fine-grained mask calibration, improving defect realization and local control. QBG injects object structure from a matched normal image into mask diffusion to produce object-aware priors, while ISC restricts anomaly propagation and preserves normal content during sampling. A lightweight materialization step recovers pixel-level labels aligned with the realized defects. On MVTec AD and VisA, OSAGEN achieves AP-P/F1-P scores of 88.1/82.2 and 68.5/66.1, respectively, under a unified downstream localization protocol. The code will be released upon acceptance.

</details>


### [63] [The K-Space Signature: Frequency-Domain Representation Learning for Medical Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29541)
*Riccardo Raciti,Francesco Guarnera,Francesco Rundo,Luca Guarnera,Sebastiano Battiato*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出K-Space Signature (KSS)框架，通过频域分析（Log-PSD空间）隔离医学图像的硬件与生成痕迹，并结合3D MLP-Mixer架构与ArcFace度量学习头，实现多中心3D MRI数据上超过0.99的准确率与ROC-AUC，且具备对未知扫描仪的零样本泛化能力（最高0.93准确率）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学影像中广泛使用生成模型合成数据以增强有限数据集，但这些合成图像可能被恶意利用制造医学深度伪造（Medical Deepfakes），威胁公共健康。现有检测方法缺乏针对频域全局光谱伪影的有效手段，且CNN存在局部空间偏置，难以处理全局分布的伪影。

Method: 1) 将分析迁移至频域，在Log-PSD空间中减去经验性全局解剖先验，抑制宏观解剖方差；2) 提取K-Space Signature表征，隔离硬件与生成痕迹；3) 提出3D MLP-Mixer架构，避免CNN的局部空间偏置，配合ArcFace度量学习头进行检测。

Result: 在多中心3D MRI数据集上，对多生成器合成数据达到超过0.99的准确率和ROC-AUC；在完全未见扫描仪的独立数据集上实现零样本泛化，保持最高0.93的判别准确率。

Conclusion: KSS框架通过频域分析与MLP-Mixer的结合，在医学深度伪造检测上实现了卓越性能与良好的跨设备泛化能力，为医学图像取证提供了可靠且可复现的解决方案。

Abstract: In medical imaging, generative models are increasingly deployed to synthesize realistic data and augment limited datasets. Unfortunately, while beneficial for privacy-preserving data sharing, these synthesized images can be repurposed for malicious intents, threatening public health through the creation of Medical Deepfakes. To address this threat, we introduce the K-Space Signature (KSS), a novel forensic framework that isolates hardware and generative traces within the spectral domain. By shifting analysis to the frequency domain, the KSS suppresses macroscopic anatomical variance by subtracting an empirical global anatomical prior computed in the Logarithmic Power Spectral Density (Log-PSD) space. To effectively process these globally distributed spectral artifacts without the local spatial bias inherent to Convolutional Neural Networks, we pair the KSS representation with a novel 3D MLP-Mixer architecture equipped with an ArcFace metric-learning head. Extensive experiments on multi-center 3D MRI datasets demonstrate that this combined approach achieves exceptional detection performance, exceeding 0.99 Accuracy and ROC-AUC on multi-generator synthetic datasets. Furthermore, the framework exhibits robust zero-shot generalization, maintaining strong discriminative power (up to 0.93 Accuracy) on independent datasets acquired from entirely unseen scanners. To ensure full reproducibility, the complete source code and pre-trained models will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


### [64] [MoRoute: Dynamic Routing for In-Context Multimodal Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29545)
*Chong Gao,Jie Ma,Zhan Peng,Chongxiao Wang,Haoxue Wu,Jun Liang,Guanbin Li,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoRoute是一个统一的视频生成框架，通过动态层路由将冻结的视觉语言模型（VLM）与预训练的视频扩散Transformer（DiT）连接起来，实现多模态条件下的视频生成与编辑，在三个基准上均超越现有最佳方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态视频生成需要在单一模型中处理文本、图像和视频的任意组合条件，这要求模型具备多模态理解能力。现有方法要么只从VLM的最终层或少量手工选择的层注入特征，要么需要联合训练架构匹配的理解与生成流，难以复用异构的预训练骨干网络。因此需要一种有效的机制来桥接VLM与视频DiT。

Method: MoRoute将冻结的VLM和预训练的视频DiT视为异构专家，通过动态层路由连接。具体包括：(1) 轻量级块级路由器使每个DiT块能够选择最相关的VLM层特征，学习多模态理解与视频合成之间的自适应对应关系；(2) 通过统一的上下文内条件注入方式，将参考图像和源视频直接融入DiT的token序列，保留细粒度视觉细节。

Result: 在IntelligentVBench、OpenVE-Bench和RefVIE-Bench三个基准上，MoRoute均超越最佳竞争方法，平均分数分别提升0.15、0.18和0.34（1-5分制）。

Conclusion: 动态层路由策略能够有效桥接异构的VLM与视频DiT骨干网络，实现多模态条件下的视频生成与编辑，验证了通过可学习路由机制自适应对齐理解和生成模块的有效性。

Abstract: Multimodal video generation aims to generate and edit videos conditioned on arbitrary combinations of text, images, and videos within a single model, allowing diverse tasks to share complementary data and generative priors. Unifying these tasks requires multimodal understanding of diverse conditions, which is typically provided by a pretrained vision-language model (VLM). A key challenge is how to connect the VLM's hierarchical multimodal representations with a pretrained video diffusion transformer (DiT). Existing methods either inject features from only the final or a few manually selected VLM layers, or jointly train architecture-matched understanding and generation streams, making it difficult to reuse heterogeneous pretrained backbones. We introduce MoRoute, a unified multimodal video generation framework that formulates a frozen VLM and a pretrained video DiT with different architectures as heterogeneous experts connected through dynamic layer routing. For each input, a lightweight block-wise router enables every DiT block to select the VLM layer most relevant to its generation stage, thereby learning an adaptive correspondence between multimodal understanding and video synthesis. MoRoute further incorporates reference images and source videos directly into the DiT token sequence through unified in-context conditioning, preserving fine-grained visual details across diverse generation and editing tasks. Experiments on IntelligentVBench, OpenVE-Bench, and RefVIE-Bench show that MoRoute consistently surpasses the best competing method on each benchmark, improving the average score by 0.15, 0.18, and 0.34 on a 1-5 scale, respectively.

</details>


### [65] [DynoDINO: Harnessing Dynamic Latent Information from DINO Features for Multi-Phase Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29568)
*Yu-Pu Hsu,Jen-Jee Chen,Yu-Chee Tseng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DynoDINO，一个面向多相位对比增强CT（CECT）图像分割的统一框架，通过切片级对齐、混合注意力机制和自适应门控机制来解决跨相位的解剖对齐和时序推理问题，在三个大规模数据集上验证了其在标准、偏移和缺失相位条件下的优越分割性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多相位CECT在病灶诊断中至关重要，但现有分割方法通常独立处理每个相位或采用简单融合策略，缺乏跨相位的时序推理能力，无法有效应对相位间错位、呼吸运动和采集不完整等问题。

Method: DynoDINO框架包含两部分：首先进行切片级对齐以建立跨相位解剖对应关系；然后采用多相位融合模型增强时序相关性，其中包含Mix-attention（MA）机制用于高效多相位特征校准，以及基于差分残差学习的自适应门控机制，选择性保留诊断相关的对比度变化同时抑制错位伪影。

Result: 在LiTS、PLC-CECT和WAW-TACE三个大规模数据集上的实验表明，DynoDINO在标准、相位偏移和相位缺失条件下均能一致地改善边界勾勒和结构保真度。

Conclusion: DynoDINO通过显式建模跨相位时序关联和选择性保留诊断相关信息，有效解决了多相位医学图像分割中错位与时序信息不连续的核心挑战，为多相位CECT分割提供了统一的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-phase Contrast-Enhanced Computed Tomography (CECT) plays a central role in the diagnosis and characterization of focal lesions by capturing temporal enhancement patterns across multiple acquisition phases. Accurate lesion segmentation from such data remains challenging because clinically relevant contrast kinetics are distributed across phases, while anatomical inconsistencies, respiratory motion, and incomplete acquisitions often lead to inter-phase misalignment and interrupted temporal information. Conventional segmentation frameworks typically process each phase independently or rely on simple fusion strategies, limiting their temporal reasoning capability.
  To address these challenges, we propose DynoDINO, a unified framework tailored to address the core challenges of multi-phase medical image segmentation. DynoDINO first performs slice-level alignment to establish inter-phase anatomical correspondence and then employs a Multi-phase Fusion Model to jointly enhance temporal correlations across phases. Our fusion model incorporates a Mix-attention (MA) mechanism for efficient multi-phase feature calibration and an Adaptive Gating Mechanism with difference-based residual learning to selectively preserve diagnostically relevant contrast variations while suppressing artifacts caused by residual misalignment. In addition, the adaptive gating mechanism improves training stability by preventing feature degradation caused by unguided subtraction operations.
  Experiments on three large-scale datasets, including LiTS, PLC-CECT, and WAW-TACE, demonstrate that DynoDINO consistently improves boundary delineation and structural fidelity under standard, shifted, and missing-phase conditions.

</details>


### [66] [Explaining AI-Image Detection: What the Heatmap Actually Shows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29581)
*Leonid Kuturin,Ilya Sotnikov,Mark Khusnutdinov,Mikhail Potemkin,Pavel Baranas,Aleksandra Korepanova,Alexander Kalashnikov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究电商平台评论照片的AI生成伪造检测问题，构建了检测器并附加归因图作为证据，在186,527张图片上通过受控实验评估。研究发现压缩历史而非合成方式主导了朴素评估性能，将一个类别对齐后检测器性能大幅下降；通过统一的最终编码可修复该问题。在归因图方面，作者提出检测器盲控（detector-blind control）作为因果评估方法，发现大多数现有归因图无法通过该控制，最后提出集成区域归因图在编辑和生成两类图像上均通过控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商平台评论照片作为退款凭证，生成模型已将伪造成本降至零，亟需可靠的检测方法与可解释证据。现有检测器在朴素评估中表现看似优异，但缺乏对真实部署中格式、压缩等差异的考量；归因图作为证据的可靠性也未得到严格因果验证。

Method: 在186,527张图像上构建伪造检测器，设置多项受控实验（产品不相交划分、重新编码合成图像为真实格式、单类对齐、三种子随机因子实验等）以识别影响性能的关键变量；同时提出检测器盲控（不查询检测器预测的控制组）来因果评估归因图的可靠性，比较17种归因映射方法，并提出集成区域归因图。

Result: 最强检测器在产品不相交分割上达到0.9999 PR-AUC，但将合成图重新编码为真实格式后降至0.7254，而五个公开检测器最多仅波动0.07。单类对齐仅移动线索而非移除；统一最终编码可修复并贡献全部增益（+0.176±0.009 PR-AUC），但仅对最后阶段有效，取证特征仍以0.7145对0.254基线率分离两类。归因图上，17种方法中只有12种（编辑图）和8种（生成图）通过检测器盲控，扰动法领先，梯度CAM无正优势；集成区域归因图在两类图像上均通过控制，像素AP最高，单图12.4秒。

Conclusion: 压缩历史而非合成方式是检测性能的主导变量；通过检测器盲控不是忠实解释的充分条件，现有方法中尚无归因图能提供完全忠实的解释。检测与归因证据的鲁棒性都需要在更真实的部署条件下进行严格验证。

Abstract: A marketplace review photograph is a document: platforms approve refunds on it, and generative models drove the cost of forging one to zero. We study that detection problem, so we build a detector and attach an attribution map as its evidence, then measure what that pair delivers on 186,527 images under controls designed to change our conclusions when something is wrong. Compression history, not synthesis, drives naive evaluation: our strongest model reaches 0.9999 PR-AUC (area under the precision-recall curve) on a product-disjoint split, yet falls to 0.7254 once we re-encode synthetics into the real class's format, while five public detectors move by at most 0.07. Aligning one class relocates the cue rather than removing it, and the repaired model then assigns native files a median probability of synthesis of 0.0004. One identical final encode for both classes repairs that, and a three-seed factorial credits the encoding change with the whole gain (+0.176 +- 0.009 PR-AUC). That encode equalises the last stage only: forensic features alone still separate the classes at 0.7145 against a base rate of 0.254. For evidence we test maps causally, against controls that never consult the detector. Whether an attribution ranking exists at all depends on whether the detector reacts to the image. On our first-fix detector, which calls 96 of 100 edited frames real, no map beats a random one. On the detector we selected, twelve of seventeen maps clear that control on edited images and eight on generated ones; perturbation leads both axes and no gradient-CAM variant shows a positive advantage. The trivial controls never clear it, and on generated images the centre prior is worse than random. Our ensembled regional map clears both axes and takes the top pixel AP at 12.4 s per map against 44.9 for occlusion. Clearing a detector-blind control is not yet a faithful explanation, and we demonstrate none.

</details>


### [67] [TraceViT: Grounded Trace Supervision for Visual Abstract Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29586)
*Binnan Liu,Yechi Ma,Tian Xie,Wei Hua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TraceViT是一种循环视觉推理器，通过在训练中引入语义单调变换链（逐步分解程序化任务解决方案得到中间网格状态），并采用软轨迹对齐（soft trace alignment）让模型自由分配迭代次数，同时结合任务参考和对象工作区进行接地，实现了对ARC基准上未知变换的推理。在ARC-AGI-1上达到67.8% pass@2，在ARC-AGI-2上达到24.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ARC任务要求模型从少量输入输出样例中推断未见过的变换并应用到新网格。循环视觉推理器虽能通过多次迭代逐步精炼预测，但传统训练只约束最终输出，中间精炼步骤缺乏监督信号。作者提出中间精炼应逐步遵循变换过程，以提升模型在ARC上的推理能力。

Method: 1) 通过重写和验证程序化任务实现，将每个解决方案分解为中间网格状态，构建语义单调变换链；2) 每次迭代由任务参考（来自少样本演示）和对象工作区（表示当前网格状态）接地；3) 由于变换链长度可能与循环迭代次数不同，采用软轨迹对齐仅强制排序约束，允许模型自由分配迭代次数。

Result: TraceViT在ARC-AGI-1上达到67.8% pass@2，在ARC-AGI-2上达到24.3%。受控消融实验表明，轨迹监督仅在配合接地机制时才有收益。

Conclusion: 语义单调变换链的轨迹监督配合接地机制能显著提升循环视觉推理器在ARC上的表现，且灵活的软轨迹对齐允许模型自适应分配迭代资源。

Abstract: The Abstraction and Reasoning Corpus (ARC) tests whether a model can infer an unseen transformation from a few input-output examples and apply it to a new grid. Looped visual reasoners refine predictions over multiple iterations, but conventional training constrains only the final output, leaving intermediate refinements unconstrained. We propose that these refinements should instead follow the transformation step by step. We introduce TraceViT, a looped visual reasoner trained with semantically monotonic transformation chains. We obtain these chains by rewriting and verifying programmatic task implementations, decomposing each solution into intermediate grid states. Each iteration is grounded by a task reference derived from the few-shot demonstrations and an object workspace representing the current grid state. Because these chains may differ in length from the loop, soft trace alignment enforces only their ordering, letting the model allocate iterations freely. TraceViT achieves 67.8% pass@2 on ARC-AGI-1 and 24.3% on ARC-AGI-2. Controlled ablations on ARC-AGI-1 show that trace supervision becomes beneficial only when paired with grounding. Code and data will be available at https://github.com/LiuBinnan/TraceViT.

</details>


### [68] [TOOD: Task-Aware Out-of-Distribution Score Calibration for Continual Learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29592)
*Mostafa ElAraby,Samer B. Nashed,Liam Paull*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了持续学习(CL)系统中的分布外(OOD)检测问题，发现OOD性能随时间下降（称为OOD遗忘OODF）与分类性能仅弱反相关，表明二者机制不同。能量型检测器因"置信间隙"（logit尺度下降）而退化，特征型检测器因"流形拥挤"而退化。作者提出TOOD，一种无需训练的后处理方法，通过按任务分解logit能量分数并利用重放缓冲区统计信息重新校准，在多个基准上显著提升OOD检测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续学习系统的核心挑战是在学习新任务的同时保持旧任务性能，但OOD检测能力（区分训练分布外的输入）同样重要却研究不足。现有CL系统在持续学习过程中OOD检测性能会显著退化，然而这一退化（OOD遗忘）的机理尚不明确，也缺乏有效的缓解策略。

Method: 作者首先系统分析了OOD遗忘的动态特性，发现OODF与已有任务分类性能仅弱反相关，说明其背后机制不同。进一步识别出两种退化效应：能量型检测器的"置信间隙"（logit尺度随任务增加而下降）和特征型检测器的"流形拥挤"。基于此提出TOOD，一种无需训练的post-hoc方法，将logit分解为按任务的能量分数，并利用重放缓冲区统计数据进行重新校准。在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100及100任务ImageNet-1K流上进行了评估。

Result: TOOD在大多数设置下优于未校准的能量方法，在十个CIFAR配置中的九个排名第一或第二，在置信间隙最严重时取得最大增益。实验结果支持核心观点：持续学习中OOD性能退化很大程度源于分数校准偏差，而非判别结构完全丧失。

Conclusion: 持续学习系统中的OOD遗忘主要由分数校准问题引起而非判别特征丢失，通过TOOD这种轻量级的post-hoc重校准方法可以显著缓解OOD退化。这一发现为CL系统中OOD检测的改进提供了新的理论视角和实用工具。

Abstract: The primary challenge of continual learning (CL) systems is to learn new tasks while remaining performant on previously learned tasks. A similarly important though less well-studied aspect of CL systems is their ability to distinguish inputs that are unlikely to come from within the set of tasks the system has already encountered, often called out-of-distribution (OOD) detection. This paper presents several findings related to the dynamics of OOD detection in CL systems, causes of performance degradation over time which we call OOD forgetting (OODF), and proposed mitigation strategies for this degradation. Chiefly, we find the unintuitive result that OODF is only weakly anti-correlated with classification performance on previous tasks, suggesting that the underlying mechanisms producing OODF are distinct. Moreover, this effect is observed for both energy-based and feature-based OOD detection methods. Energy-based detectors suffer a drop in logit scale as additional tasks are learned, which we term the Confidence Gap, while feature-based detectors also degrade under a complementary effect we call Manifold Crowding. Motivated by these observations, we propose TOOD, a training-free post-hoc method that decomposes logits into per-task energy scores and re-calibrates them using replay-buffer statistics. Experiments on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and a 100-task ImageNet-1K stream show that TOOD improves OOD detection performance over uncalibrated energy in most settings and ranks first or second in nine of ten CIFAR configurations, with the largest gains when the confidence gap is most severe. These results suggest that a substantial portion of OOD deterioration in continual learning arises from score miscalibration rather than from a complete loss of discriminative structure.

</details>


### [69] [CoDe-SSM: Context-Detail Decoupled State Space Model for Efficient UHD Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29595)
*Jiaxu Su,Zhijian Wu,Jun Li,Bo Zhang,Yefeng Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CoDe-SSM（上下文-细节解耦状态空间模型），用于超高清（UHD）图像复原。该模型将聚合上下文与聚类残差分离到两条路径中处理：GCSM（全局簇扫描模块）通过K个输入相关的簇中心进行选择性SSM推理实现跨区域上下文共享，LHFM（局部高频模块）利用输入推导的高频掩码和稀疏卷积专家混合来处理聚类残差。在五个UHD基准和五种退化类型上验证了该解耦策略在保持高效性的同时显著提升复原质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: UHD图像复原需要在聚合空间重复出现的退化线索与保留局部图像结构之间取得平衡。现有的基于下采样、窗口划分或聚类token缩减的方法虽能控制计算成本，但大多未显式保留聚合中难以表征的细节信息，导致边缘、纹理等精细结构受损。

Method: 提出上下文-细节解耦的双路径架构。上下文建模路径采用GCSM，将特征聚合成K个输入相关的簇中心，对固定顺序序列应用选择性SSM推理，实现跨区域上下文共享并将计算量与空间分辨率解耦；细节恢复路径采用LHFM，使用输入推导的高频掩码和稀疏混合卷积专家处理聚类残差。

Result: 在五个UHD基准和五种退化类型（含去噪、去模糊、去雨等）上的大量实验表明，显式的上下文-细节解耦策略在保持理想效率的同时获得了可观的复原质量提升。

Conclusion: CoDe-SSM通过将聚合上下文与高频细节分离处理，有效解决了UHD图像复原中全局信息聚合与局部结构保留的矛盾，证明了显式解耦策略的性能优势与效率可行性。

Abstract: Ultra-high-definition (UHD) image restoration must balance the aggregation of spatially recurring degradation cues with the preservation of localized image structures. Compact aggregation can reduce redundant processing but may attenuate edges, textures, and other fine structures. Existing approaches manage UHD restoration cost through downsampling, window partitioning, or cluster-based token reduction; yet many of them do not explicitly retain information that is poorly represented by shared aggregation. In this study, we propose a Context-Detail Decoupled State Space Model (CoDe-SSM) for UHD restoration, which processes aggregated context and clustering residuals in separate pathways. The context modeling pathway, implemented by the Global Cluster Scan Module (GCSM), aggregates features into $K$ input-dependent cluster centers and applies selective SSM reasoning over the resulting fixed-order sequence, enabling cross-region context sharing while decoupling computational cost from spatial resolution. The detail recovery pathway, implemented by the Local High-Frequency Module (LHFM), processes the clustering residual with an input-derived high-frequency mask and a sparse mixture of convolutional experts. Extensive experiments on five UHD benchmarks and five degradation types demonstrate that our explicit context-detail decoupling strategy yields substantial gains in restoration quality while maintaining desirable efficiency.

</details>


### [70] [FlexComposer: Unified Video Compositing from Images to Dynamic Footage with Flexible Trajectory Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29627)
*Songchun Zhang,Sitong Guo,Xianghao Kong,Pengwei Liu,Yuwei Guo,Lvmin Zhang,Anyi Rao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: FlexComposer是一个统一的视频合成框架，将视频合成标准化为轨迹引导的条件生成任务，通过统一前景表示、空间感知潜空间注入和合成到真实数据的课程学习三大设计，实现了静态图像和动态视频素材的无缝集成，在视觉质量、时间一致性和轨迹遵循方面均优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式视频合成方法存在控制保真度权衡问题：要么从静态图像幻觉运动，无法保留预动画素材的动态；要么缺乏细粒度的空间控制，无法沿用户定义轨迹精确放置素材。需要一种统一框架同时解决运动保持和空间控制的问题。

Method: FlexComposer提出三个关键设计：(1) 统一规范前景表示，将物体内在运动与全局位移解耦，标准化异构输入为稳定的居中潜空间；(2) 空间感知潜注入策略，利用VAE潜空间的平移等变性，通过无参数机制将规范特征传输到目标轨迹上；(3) 混合数据集与合成到真实课程学习，结合程序化仿真、真实影视素材和生成数据，隐式学习物理上合理的光照和阴影协调。

Result: 通过大量实验证明，FlexComposer在视觉质量、时间一致性和轨迹遵循三个维度上均优于现有最先进方法，能处理从产品照片到动态主体的多样化输入，无需显式3D重建或辅助可学习适配器。

Conclusion: FlexComposer通过统一的轨迹引导条件生成框架，成功弥合了控制保真度的权衡，实现了高质量、高精度的视频合成，为生成式视频合成提供了一种无需额外适配器的通用解决方案。

Abstract: Generative video compositing, which involves inserting external assets seamlessly into existing video sequences, is essential for content creation and visual effects. However, existing approaches suffer from a control-fidelity trade-off: they either hallucinate motion from static images, failing to preserve the dynamics of pre-animated assets, or lack fine-grained spatial control for precise asset placement along user-defined trajectories. We propose FlexComposer, a unified framework that standardizes video compositing as a trajectory-guided conditional generation task, enabling the seamless integration of both static images and dynamic footage. Our approach introduces three key designs: (1) a Unified Canonical Foreground Representation that decouples an object's intrinsic motion from its global displacement, standardizing heterogeneous inputs into a stabilized, centered latent space; (2) a Spatial-Aware Latent Injection strategy that exploits the translation equivariance of VAE latent spaces to transport canonical features onto target trajectories via a parameter-free mechanism; and (3) a Hybrid Dataset and Synthetic-to-Real Curriculum that synergizes procedural simulation, real-world cinematic footage, and generative data to implicitly learn physically plausible illumination and shadow harmonization. This unified design handles diverse inputs from product photos to dynamic subjects achieving high-fidelity motion control and environmental integration without the need for explicit 3D reconstruction or auxiliary learnable adapters. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FlexComposer outperforms state-of-the-art methods in visual quality, temporal consistency, and trajectory adherence.

</details>


### [71] [OASIS: Occlusion-aware Single-image Hand Avatar Reconstruction via 3D Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29633)
*Zhisheng Han,Shiyao Wu,Jiayan Qiu,Yakun Ju,Lu Liu,Le Zhang,Pengfei Feng,Huiyu Zhou,Zheheng Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: OASIS是一个基于3D高斯泼溅的单图像手部头像重建框架，通过几何对齐的视觉证据token、可见性条件点-图像注意力以及Feature-on-Mesh表示来应对自遮挡和姿势相关变形等挑战，在视觉保真度和效率上优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单图像3D手部头像重建本质上是病态问题，由于严重的自遮挡导致视觉证据有限，且高度关节化的手部存在复杂的姿势相关变形。现有方法主要依赖隐式NeRF风格表示，其体素拟合计算开销大且难以保留细粒度手部细节。

Method: (1) 构建几何对齐的视觉证据token，将输入图像观测与3D手部几何显式对齐并进行上下文自适应token化；(2) 提出可见性条件点-图像注意力机制，可靠地将视觉证据传递给几何token，生成遮挡感知的高斯特征；(3) 引入Feature-on-Mesh表示，使高斯变形由局部表面拉伸引导以捕捉非刚性变形；(4) 采用一次性适应方案，从多身份训练数据学习共享手部先验，再拟合到目标图像进行特定重建。

Result: 在挑战性姿态和野外场景中，OASIS在视觉保真度和效率上均优于现有基线，并在文本到头像生成、纹理编辑等下游应用中展现出强通用性。

Conclusion: OASIS通过3D高斯泼溅框架有效解决了单图像手部头像重建中自遮挡和姿势变形两大核心挑战，在实现高质量重建的同时显著提升效率，并为下游应用提供了良好的扩展性。

Abstract: Single-image 3D hand avatar reconstruction is fundamentally ill-posed and particularly challenging due to limited visual evidence under severe self-occlusion and the complex pose-dependent deformation of highly articulated hands. Existing methods predominantly rely on implicit NeRF-style representations, whose volumetric fitting is computationally expensive and often struggles to preserve fine-grained hand details. In this work, we present OASIS, a tailored 3D Gaussian Splatting framework for single-image hand avatar reconstruction. To faithfully encode sparse image-specific appearance cues in single-view reconstruction, we construct geometry-aligned visual evidence tokens by explicitly aligning input image observations with 3D hand geometry and context-adaptively tokenizing the resulting visual evidence. Since severe self-occlusion makes the reliability of image evidence inherently visibility-dependent, we introduce a visibility-conditioned point-image attention to reliably transfer visual evidence to geometric tokens, yielding occlusion-aware Gaussian features for faithful and robust reconstruction. To further capture non-rigid deformation of articulated hands, we introduce a Feature-on-Mesh representation to enable Gaussian deformation to be guided by local surface stretching. Under this framework, we adopt a one-shot adaptation scheme that learns a shared hand prior from multi-identity training data and then fits it to a target image for target-specific reconstruction. Extensive experiments show that OASIS outperforms existing baselines in both visual fidelity and efficiency across challenging poses and in-the-wild scenarios, and further demonstrates strong versatility in downstream applications such as text-to-avatar generation and texture editing.

</details>


### [72] [CodeShrink: Adaptive Visual Compression for Efficient Multimodal Code Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29637)
*Wenxin Tang,Jingyu Xiao,Zhenyu Liu,Zipeng Xie,Junliang Liu,Wang Luo,Yuan Jiang,Yintong Huo,Michael Lyu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CodeShrink是一个自适应视觉压缩框架，通过三种组件（空白消除渲染、基于强化学习的自适应压缩配置、指令感知的显著Token选择）来减少多模态大语言模型处理代码图像时的视觉Token开销，在代码问答、克隆检测和代码补全任务上实现最高71.2%的Token缩减，同时保持甚至超越未压缩基线性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将源代码渲染为图像是降低MLLM输入成本的有效途径，但现有的分辨率缩放策略存在两处低效：一是由换行和缩进产生的空白区域导致不必要的视觉Token；二是与当前指令无关的代码区域被无差别处理。此外，最优压缩设置因输入、任务和模型而异，固定的压缩比例策略难以适用所有场景。

Method: CodeShrink包含三个核心组件：(1) Blank-Free Rendering，以紧凑布局和显式结构标记替代依赖空白的布局，消除由布局引起的Token开销；(2) Adaptive Compression Configuration，使用强化学习训练的轻量级智能体预测每个输入的自适应压缩设置，在Token效率与可读性之间取得平衡；(3) Dominant Token Selection，推理阶段联合分析指令和代码图像，裁剪与任务无关的视觉Token。

Result: 在代码问答、克隆检测和代码补全三个任务上的实验表明，CodeShrink将视觉Token使用量减少最高71.2%，同时匹配或超越未压缩的纯文本输入表现，并一致优于基于文本和基于视觉的压缩基线。

Conclusion: 布局压缩、自适应配置和指令感知剪枝的组合方法能够有效提升多模态代码理解的效率，为MLLM中的视觉代码表示提供了实用的优化方案。

Abstract: Rendering source code as images offers a promising way to reduce the input costs of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). Adjusting image resolution can trade visual token cost against content fidelity. However, resolution scaling alone overlooks two sources of inefficiency: blank regions created by line breaks and indentation, and code regions irrelevant to the current instruction. Moreover, the best compression setting varies across inputs, tasks, and models, limiting fixed-ratio strategies. We propose CodeShrink, an adaptive visual compression framework with three components. Blank-Free Rendering replaces whitespace-dependent layouts with compact layouts and explicit structural markers, removing layout-induced tokens. Adaptive Compression Configuration uses a lightweight agent trained with reinforcement learning to predict a per-input setting that balances token efficiency and readability. Dominant Token Selection jointly analyzes the instruction and code image to prune task-irrelevant visual tokens during inference. We evaluate CodeShrink on code question answering, clone detection, and code completion. CodeShrink reduces visual token use by up to 71.2\% while matching or exceeding uncompressed text-only inputs, and consistently outperforms text-based and visual compression baselines across all three tasks. These results show that combining layout compaction, adaptive configuration, and instruction-aware pruning can make multimodal code understanding more efficient. Our code is available at https://github.com/vinsontang1/CodeShrink.

</details>


### [73] [HierDoc: Hierarchical Page-to-Region Evidence Routing for Long-Document Visual Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29638)
*Rongjian Gu,Wengang Zhou,Junyu Xiong,Yonghui Wang,Bing Yin,Bei Wang,Houqiang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出HierDoc，一种层次化证据路由框架，将长文档证据获取形式化为从页面到区域的两阶段集合预测，通过阶段式GRPO优化策略，实现页面级与区域级证据选择的联合统一，在多页文档VQA基准上取得SOTA或竞争性结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在页级和区域级证据选择上存在割裂：页级方法将区域操作仅作为导航辅助，而区域级方法假设相关页面已给定。这种断开导致无法将页面和区域选择视为连续的证据决策，缺乏统一的证据获取流程。

Method: HierDoc采用层次化证据路由：页面策略从全文档中选择证据页面，然后解析这些页面的语义元素，再由区域策略选取传递给下游答案模型的元素。两个策略均与答案无关，使用阶段式GRPO和粒度特定的结构化集合奖励进行优化。答案模型同时接收选定全页、区域裁剪以及OCR或表格文本，兼顾全局上下文与细粒度证据。

Result: 在多个基准上达到开放权重系统中的SOTA或竞争性能，LongDocURL相对最强开放权重基线提升16.87%；受控消融显示，区域证据选择使仅页面系统在准确率和F1上分别提升5.51%和4.82%。

Conclusion: 将粗粒度页面路由和细粒度区域路由组织为统一证据获取过程中的连续、独立优化阶段，能显著提升长文档VQA的证据定位与回答性能。

Abstract: Multi-page document visual question answering requires locating sparse evidence at both the page and region levels. Existing approaches typically emphasize one level over the other: page-centric methods focus on page acquisition, with region operations serving mainly as navigation aids, whereas region-centric methods assume that the relevant pages have already been supplied. Consequently, page and region selection remain disconnected rather than forming successive evidence decisions. We propose HierDoc, a hierarchical evidence-routing framework that formulates long-document evidence acquisition as two-stage set prediction from pages to regions. A page policy selects evidence pages from the full document; these pages are then parsed for semantic elements, after which a region policy selects the elements passed to a downstream answer model. Both answer-agnostic policies are optimized with stage-wise GRPO using granularity-specific structured-set rewards. The answer model receives selected full pages together with selected region crops and OCR or table text, preserving global context while emphasizing fine-grained evidence. Across the evaluated benchmarks, HierDoc achieves state-of-the-art or competitive performance among open-weight systems, improving LongDocURL by 16.87% relative to the strongest reported open-weight baseline. Controlled ablations further show that selected regional evidence improves the page-only system in accuracy and F1 by 5.51% and 4.82%, respectively. These results demonstrate the benefit of organizing coarse page routing and fine-grained region routing as successive, separately optimized stages of a unified evidence-acquisition process.

</details>


### [74] [Scaling Properties of Text Conditioning in Visual Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29679)
*Zilong Chen,Chaorui Deng,Kunchang Li,Hongyi Yuan,Haoqi Fan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了文本条件在视觉生成中的经验缩放特性。作者发现扩散损失并非随自然语言提示的token数缩放，而是与提示中"结构化语言"的量相关。他们采用两个互补指标（白盒似然指标GPG和黑盒属性指标ED）来量化结构化语言，发现收敛的扩散损失随GPG近似线性下降，随ED呈幂律关系。基于这些缩放特性，作者通过构造带语义和几何标注的结构化提示来提升"可扩散性"，并通过监督微调、冷启动和验证器门控的在线策略蒸馏训练提示器来提升"可提示性"。最终系统在几乎所有组合、推理和世界知识基准上超越所有开源权重模型，并在大多数评估上匹配或超越最强闭源模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本条件在视觉生成中的缩放特性很少被量化研究，因为扩散损失在自然语言提示下不随token数量缩放。理解结构化语言与扩散损失之间的缩放关系，可以为构建更好的提示系统和训练策略提供理论指导。

Method: 1) 提出两个互补指标量化提示中的结构化语言：白盒似然指标GPG和黑盒属性指标ED；2) 通过受控训练实验测量缩放特性，发现扩散损失随GPG近似线性下降、随ED呈幂律下降；3) 基于这些特性，构造带语义和几何标注的结构化提示提升可扩散性；4) 通过监督微调、冷启动和验证器门控的在线策略蒸馏训练提示器（prompter）提升可提示性。

Result: 系统在几乎所有组合、推理和世界知识基准上超越所有评估的开源权重模型，并在大多数评估上匹配或超越最强闭源模型。

Conclusion: 文本条件在视觉生成中存在可测量的缩放规律：结构化语言的数量决定了扩散损失的下界。利用这一规律，可以通过构造结构化提示和训练专用提示器来显著提升文本到图像生成系统的性能，达到甚至超越最先进的闭源模型水平。

Abstract: We study empirical scaling properties for text conditioning in visual generation. Such properties have rarely been measured because diffusion loss does not scale with the number of tokens in natural-language prompts. Surprisingly, we find that the converged diffusion loss scales with the amount of structured language in the prompt. To quantify structured language, we adapt two complementary measures: a white-box likelihood metric (GPG) and a black-box attribute metric (ED). Across controlled training runs, the converged diffusion loss decreases approximately linearly with GPG and follows a power law with ED. Guided by these scaling properties, we improve \emph{diffusability} by constructing structured prompts with semantic and geometric annotations derived from images, and improve \emph{promptability} by training a prompter through supervised fine-tuning, cold-start, and verifier-gated on-policy distillation. The resulting system outperforms all evaluated open-weight models on nearly every compositional, reasoning, and world-knowledge benchmark, while matching or surpassing the strongest closed-weight models on most evaluations.

</details>


### [75] [Toward Robust and 3D-Aware RGB-NIR Imaging in the Dark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.29684)
*Muyao Niu,Mingze Ma,Yifan Zhan,Qingtian Zhu,Zhihang Zhong,Wei Guo,Chang Wen Chen,Yinqiang Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需干净RGB监督的3D感知神经建模方法，用于RGB-NIR低光图像增强。通过将极噪RGB与NIR线索在3D空间隐式融合，模型无需成对训练数据即可恢复干净RGB图像，且对不同噪声水平具有泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低光RGB-NIR融合方法依赖精心标注的训练数据对（clean RGB supervision），在不同场景下鲁棒性有限。作者希望摆脱对干净RGB数据收集的依赖，提升模型跨噪声水平的泛化能力。

Method: 采用3D感知神经建模，将极噪RGB观测与NIR线索在3D空间中进行隐式融合优化，无需干净RGB监督信号即可训练出强大的模型，从而恢复出干净的RGB图像。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据上进行的大量实验表明，所提方法优于现有方法，具有更好的泛化性能和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 通过引入3D感知建模视角，成功消除了对干净RGB数据配对的依赖，实现了更鲁棒、可泛化的RGB-NIR低光图像增强。

Abstract: Robust low-light imaging remains challenging for the community. Recent studies have explored fusing Near-Infrared (NIR) with noisy RGB to achieve improved enhancement, yet most methods depend on carefully curated training data pairs, with limited robustness under different scenarios. This paper offers a new perspective for RGB-NIR low-light imaging by incorporating 3D-aware neural modeling. Without using clean RGB supervision, a powerful model can be optimized to implicitly fuse extremely noisy RGB observations with NIR cues in 3D space, effectively recovering clean RGB images. The proposed model obviates the requirement for clean RGB data collection, generalizes across different noise levels. Extensive evaluations on synthetic and real data demonstrate its superiority. Codes available: https://github.com/MyNiuuu/3DarkFusion

</details>

### [76] [Can LLMs Really Understand Item Difficulty Levels? Implications for Automated Item Generation Using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28634)
*Xinyi Wang,Hong Jiao,Ming Li,Sydney Peters,Hanna Choi,Tianyi Zhou,Qingshu Xu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了大型语言模型（LLMs）在预测大规模阅读与写作测试中题目难度等级方面的表现。结果显示，零样本GPT-4.1（温度为0）在LLM中表现最佳（QWK=0.578），但仍不及ConvBERT（QWK=0.625）。研究发现LLMs难以识别困难题目，且GPT-5.4倾向于低估题目难度，表明仅凭语义信息可能不足以准确预测题目难度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 题目难度估计在形成性评估和大型高风险总结性评估中至关重要，本研究旨在探索LLMs在预测题目难度等级方面的能力，为评估领域提供LLMs应用的实证依据。

Method: 使用来自大规模阅读与写作测试的题目，在多个LLMs上探索不同的提示策略和参数设置，将LLM性能与编码器语言模型（如ConvBERT）及基于特征的监督机器学习模型进行比较，并通过嵌入降维分析语义信息与难度等级的关系。

Result: 零样本GPT-4.1（温度0）获得最高LLM预测准确率（QWK=0.578），但低于ConvBERT（QWK=0.625）。所有LLMs难以标记困难题目，GPT-5.4倾向于低估题目难度。嵌入降维显示不同难度等级的题目嵌入相互混杂。

Conclusion: LLMs在题目难度预测上的表现不如专用编码器模型，且随着自身能力增强倾向于将所有题目视为简单，因此使用LLMs生成具有目标难度等级的题目时应保持谨慎。

Abstract: The estimation of item difficulty plays a key role in both formative assessment and large-scale high-stakes summative assessments. This study explores how large language models (LLMs) perform in predicting item difficulty levels using items from a large-scale Reading and Writing test. The study investigated various prompting strategies and parameter settings across multiple LLMs. LLM performance was compared with encoder-only language models and feature-based supervised machine learning models. Zero-shot GPT-4.1 with a temperature of 0 yielded the highest item difficulty level prediction accuracy, with a quadratic weighted kappa (QWK) of 0.578. However, LLMs' prediction accuracy was lower than that of ConvBERT (QWK = 0.625), which outperformed the best feature-based supervised machine learning model. Further analysis showed that all LLMs struggled to label hard items; in particular, the current advanced GPT-5.4 tended to underestimate item difficulty levels. Dimension reduction of embeddings showed that item embeddings from different difficulty levels were mixed together, indicating that semantic information from items alone is likely insufficient for item difficulty level prediction. The findings suggest that if LLMs cannot understand item difficulty levels as evidenced by empirical data and tend to treat most items as easy when their own capabilities increase, caution should be exercised when using LLMs to generate items with targeted difficulty levels.

</details>


### [77] [Imbalanced Data Clustering via Targeted Data Augmentation Using GMM and LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28635)
*Noor Khalal,Abdallah Alaa-Eddine Djamai,Imed Keraghel,Mohamed Nadif*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新颖的无监督数据增强方法，结合高斯混合模型（GMM）和大型语言模型（LLM），通过GMM检测数据中代表性不足的簇，再利用LLM生成合成文本来丰富这些簇，从而改善无监督NLP任务中少数主题的表示。实验表明该方法在所有情况下都能保持聚类性能，且常常提升簇的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在NLP中，处理代表性不足的主题是一个挑战，尤其是在无监督任务中，聚类可能无法充分捕捉少数主题。现有方法缺乏针对无监督场景下少数主题增强的有效解决方案。

Method: 该方法将高斯混合模型（GMM）与大型语言模型（LLM）相结合：GMM凭借其灵活性和鲁棒性检测数据中对应代表性不足区域的簇，LLM则生成合成文档以丰富这些簇，改善其数据表示。

Result: 在不均衡文本数据集上的实验表明，该方法在所有情况下都能保持聚类性能，并常常增强簇的可解释性。

Conclusion: 该方法为无监督NLP任务中改善数据表示提供了一种稳健且可扩展的解决方案，能够有效提升少数主题的表示质量而不损害整体聚类性能。

Abstract: In Natural Language Processing (NLP), dealing with underrepresented topics is challenging, especially in unsupervised tasks where clustering might not adequately capture minority topics. To tackle this challenge, our paper presents a novel unsupervised data augmentation method that integrates Gaussian Mixture Models (GMMs) and Large Language Models (LLMs). Due to their flexibility and robustness, GMMs can detect clusters corresponding to underrepresented areas in the data, while LLMs create synthetic documents to enrich these clusters and improve their representation. Experiments on various imbalanced text datasets demonstrate that our approach preserves clustering performance in all cases and often enhances cluster interpretability, offering a robust and scalable solution for improving data representation in unsupervised NLP tasks.

</details>


### [78] [Chain-of-Models: Cross-Model Auditing for Bias-Robust LLM Judges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28636)
*Qian Wang,Zhanzhi Lou,Zhenheng Tang,Nuo Chen,Bingsheng He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Chain-of-Models (CoM)自动化审计管线，由第二个模型检查第一个模型的推理轨迹后再产出最终判断。研究发现审计者身份至关重要：独立的抗偏差能力不能预测审计效果，且最佳审计者是偏差特异的。基于此提出按偏差类型选择审计者的规则，在四个偏差切片上达到0.884的准确率，优于最强固定审计者（0.824）和无审计基线（0.805）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为自动评判者易受认知偏差影响，现有缓解方法要么依赖提示驱动的去偏（在不同偏差类型间不够稳健），要么依赖人工评估（无法规模化）。需要一种可自动化、跨偏差类型的审计方案。

Method: 提出Chain-of-Models (CoM)管线：一个审计模型（可能是同一模型、同族模型或异族模型）检查主模型的推理轨迹后再生成最终判断。在9个模型、6个模型家族、4种认知偏差、4个事实性数据集上进行系统实验，并设计了一个按偏差类型选择审计者的规则，综合功能多样性、按偏差独立的抗偏差能力和校准后的审计有效性三个指标进行评分。

Result: 审计者身份对审计效果有两个关键影响：(1)独立的抗偏差能力不能预测审计效果——Kimi-K2.5在多个偏差上是最强独立模型，但对Qwen2.5-72B的有偏轨迹却是弱审计者；(2)最佳审计者是偏差特异的——GPT-4o在bandwagon、authority和distraction上最强，GLM-5在sycophancy上最强。基于偏差类型的审计者选择规则在四个偏差切片上达到0.884准确率，优于最强固定审计者（0.824）和无审计基线（0.805）。

Conclusion: Chain-of-Models是一种有效的自动化审计管线，但审计者选择必须考虑偏差特异性和模型间关系，不能简单地用独立抗偏差能力来判断审计效果。提出的按偏差类型选择审计者的规则能显著提升去偏效果，且数据、配置和LLM-agent技能已开源。

Abstract: LLMs increasingly serve as automated judges, but their judgments remain vulnerable to cognitive biases. Existing mitigations mostly rely on prompt-driven debiasing, which is brittle across bias types, or human evaluation, which does not scale. We study \emph{Chain-of-Models} (CoM), an automated audit pipeline in which a second model inspects the first model's reasoning trace before producing the final judgment. The key design question is whether the auditor should be the same model, a same-family model, or a different-family model. Across 9 models from 6 families, 4 cognitive biases, and 4 factual datasets, we find that auditor identity matters in two ways. First, standalone bias resistance does not predict audit effectiveness: Kimi-K2.5 is the strongest standalone model on several biases, yet is a weak auditor for Qwen2.5-72B's biased traces. Second, the best auditor is bias-specific: GPT-4o is strongest on bandwagon, authority, and distraction, while GLM-5 is strongest on sycophancy. We operationalize these findings with a per-bias auditor selection rule that, given the bias type, scores candidates along functional diversity, per-bias standalone resistance, and calibrated audit effectiveness. Under a calibration/test split, the selector reaches the highest accuracy across the four biased slices ($0.884$ vs.\ $0.824$ for the strongest single fixed auditor and $0.805$ for the no-audit baseline). We release data, configurations, and an LLM-agent skill at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/chain-of-models-B585 .

</details>


### [79] [ZeroR@CHiPSAL 2026: Two-Stage Vision-Language Adaptation with Contrastive Learning for Nepali Meme Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28637)
*Nitiz Khanal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了CHiPSAL 2026共享任务中面向尼泊尔语模因的多模态仇恨言论与情感检测系统。基于RA-HMD框架和Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct模型，采用两阶段训练流程（LoRA微调生成式分类+对比骨干微调），通过集成策略在仇恨言论检测中获得第二名（F1: 0.797），情感分析中获得第四名（F1: 0.518）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尼泊尔语属于低资源南亚语言，模因中的多模态仇恨言论和情感检测面临缺乏标注数据、OCR和翻译管道误差传播等挑战。现有方法大多依赖分离的OCR与翻译管道，无法有效利用模型对Devanagari文字的原生理解能力。

Method: 采用两阶段训练管道：(1) LoRA微调搭配MLP投影头进行生成式分类；(2) 使用监督InfoNCE损失的对比骨干微调。处理类别不平衡采用少数类过采样、图像增强和焦点损失。推理阶段通过验证集调优的权重集成阶段1的token概率与阶段2的分类器分数。端到端方法利用Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct对Devanagari的原生理解，消除OCR和翻译管道的误差传播。

Result: 在CHiPSAL 2026共享任务中，仇恨言论检测取得第二名（F1: 0.797），情感分析取得第四名（F1: 0.518）。提供了详细的消融实验和错误分析。

Conclusion: 端到端的大视觉语言模型微调方法对低资源南亚语言的多模态理解有效，原生Devanagari支持优于分离式OCR+翻译管道，为后续低资源语言模因分析研究提供了思路和洞见。

Abstract: This paper presents our system for the CHiPSAL 2026 shared task on multimodal hate speech and sentiment detection in Nepali memes. We address both subtasks: binary hate speech classification and three-class sentiment analysis. Our approach adapts the Robust Adaptation of Hateful Meme Detection (RA-HMD) framework using Qwen3-VL-8B-Instruct, a state-of-the-art vision-language model with native Devanagari support. We employ a two-stage training pipeline: (1) LoRA fine-tuning with an MLP projection head for generative classification, and (2) contrastive backbone fine-tuning with supervised InfoNCE loss. We handle class imbalance through minority oversampling, image augmentation, and focal loss. At inference, we ensemble Stage 1 token probabilities with Stage 2 classifier scores using validation-tuned weights. Our end-to-end approach eliminates error propagation from separate OCR and translation pipelines by leveraging the model's native Devanagari understanding. Our system achieved \textbf{2nd place} on hate speech detection (F1: 0.797) and \textbf{4th place} on sentiment analysis (F1: 0.518). We provide detailed ablations, error analysis, and insights into adapting large vision-language models for low-resource South Asian languages.

</details>


### [80] [Learning Stateful Predictive Knowledge From Experience](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.28638)
*Yan Song,Xidong Feng,Bo Liu,Xinyu Cui,Haotian Fu,Zichen Liu,Mengyue Yang,Cheng Deng,Jian Zhao,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出状态知识学习（SKL）方法，将LLM智能体的学习从轨迹级反思（经验性事后回顾）转向状态锚定的显式声明性预测知识（前瞻性预测），并通过自蒸馏（SKL-SD）和强化学习（SKL-RL）两种算法实现，在WebShop、ScienceWorld和ChessPuzzles等任务上显著优于现有基于反思的训练范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体主要依赖轨迹级反思从经验中提取洞察，但从预测知识的视角来看，这种方法基于事后性回忆而非预测性前瞻，产生的启发式规则脆弱且依赖具体路径，缺乏泛化能力。

Method: 提出Stateful Knowledge Learning（SKL）框架，将智能体的学习焦点从轨迹级总结转向维护状态知识（即锚定于状态的显式声明性预测评估）。具体包含两种算法：SKL-SD（基于自蒸馏）和SKL-RL（基于强化学习），训练智能体自主从经验中提取状态grounded的预测知识，并学习将其用于策略制定。

Result: 在WebShop、ScienceWorld和ChessPuzzles三个交互式/复杂推理环境中的实验表明，赋予模型学习状态预测知识的内在能力，显著超越了当前基于反思的训练范式。此外，状态知识带来了细粒度、更好的泛化性和知识引导（bootstrapping）能力。

Conclusion: 状态级预测知识相比轨迹级反思是更优的智能体学习范式，能够实现知识复用和迭代增强，为LLM智能体的经验学习提供了新的方向。

Abstract: As large language model (LLM) agents increasingly learn from experience, they primarily rely on trajectory-level reflection to extract insights. Viewed through the lens of predictive knowledge, we argue that this approach operates on episodic hindsight rather than predictive foresight, yielding brittle, path-dependent heuristics. To address this, we propose Stateful Knowledge Learning (SKL). SKL shifts the agent's focus from trajectory-level summarization to maintaining Stateful Knowledge: explicit, declarative predictive assessments anchored to state. We first demonstrate a motivating example showing how stateful knowledge provides granularity, enhances generalization, and enables knowledge bootstrapping. To further scale up the idea, we introduce two algorithms via self-distillation (SKL-SD) and reinforcement learning (SKL-RL), training agents to autonomously extract state-grounded predictive knowledge from experience and learn to leverage it for policy making.
